    label: string
    provider: string
    baseUrl: string
    protocol: string
    defaultQuickModel?: string
    defaultDeepModel?: string
    editableBaseUrl?: boolean
}

const PROVIDER_PRESETS: ProviderPreset[] = [
    { id: 'openai', label: 'OpenAI', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://api.openai.com/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI', defaultQuickModel: 'gpt-4o-mini', defaultDeepModel: 'gpt-4o' },
    { id: 'anthropic', label: 'Anthropic', provider: 'anthropic', baseUrl: '', protocol: 'Anthropic' },
    { id: 'google', label: 'Google Gemini', provider: 'google', baseUrl: '', protocol: 'Google' },
    { id: 'dashscope', label: '阿里云百炼（DashScope）', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://dashscope.aliyuncs.com/compatible-mode/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'qwen-plus', defaultDeepModel: 'qwen-max' },
    { id: 'deepseek', label: 'DeepSeek', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://api.deepseek.com/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'deepseek-chat', defaultDeepModel: 'deepseek-reasoner' },
    { id: 'moonshot', label: 'Moonshot AI（Kimi）', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://api.moonshot.cn/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'moonshot-v1-8k', defaultDeepModel: 'moonshot-v1-32k' },
    { id: 'zhipu', label: '智谱 AI', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/paas/v4', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'glm-4.5-air', defaultDeepModel: 'glm-4.5' },
    { id: 'zhipu-coding', label: '智谱 Coding Plan', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://open.bigmodel.cn/api/coding/paas/v4', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'glm-4.5-air', defaultDeepModel: 'glm-5-turbo' },
    { id: 'xiaomi-mimo', label: '小米 MiMo', provider: 'mimo', baseUrl: 'https://api.xiaomimimo.com/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'xiaomi/mimo-v2-flash', defaultDeepModel: 'xiaomi/mimo-v2-pro' },
    { id: 'xiaomi-token-plan', label: '小米 MiMo Token Plan', provider: 'mimo', baseUrl: 'https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', defaultQuickModel: 'mimo-v2.5', defaultDeepModel: 'mimo-v2.5-pro' },
    { id: 'siliconflow', label: '硅基流动', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: 'https://api.siliconflow.cn/v1', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容' },
    { id: 'custom-openai', label: '自定义 OpenAI 兼容', provider: 'openai', baseUrl: '', protocol: 'OpenAI 兼容', editableBaseUrl: true },
]

export function inferPreset(llmProvider: string, backendUrl: string): string {
    const normalizedProvider = (llmProvider || '').toLowerCase()
    const normalizedUrl = (backendUrl || '').replace(/\/$/, '')
    if (normalizedProvider === 'openai') {
        const legacyMimoPreset = PROVIDER_PRESETS.find(
            (preset) => preset.provider === 'mimo'
                && preset.baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '') === normalizedUrl,
        )
        if (legacyMimoPreset) return legacyMimoPreset.id
    }
    const matched = PROVIDER_PRESETS.find((preset) => {
        if (preset.provider !== normalizedProvider) return false
        if (!preset.baseUrl && preset.id !== 'custom-openai') return true
        return preset.baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '') === normalizedUrl
    })
    if (matched) return matched.id
    if (normalizedProvider === 'openai') return 'custom-openai'
    return normalizedProvider || 'openai'
}

function providerKeyScope(llmProvider: string, backendUrl: string): string {
    const provider = (llmProvider || 'openai').trim().toLowerCase() || 'openai'
    const normalizedUrl = (backendUrl || '').trim().replace(/\/$/, '')
    return normalizedUrl ? `${provider}:${normalizedUrl}` : provider
}

export default function Settings() {
    const { user } = useAuthStore()
    const [defaultAnalysts, setDefaultAnalysts] = useState(['market', 'social', 'news', 'fundamentals', 'macro', 'smart_money', 'volume_price'])
    const [customPrompt, setCustomPrompt] = useState('')
    const [llmApiKey, setLlmApiKey] = useState('')
    const [hasStoredApiKey, setHasStoredApiKey] = useState(false)
    const [apiKeyScopes, setApiKeyScopes] = useState<string[]>([])
    const [wecomWebhook, setWecomWebhook] = useState('')
    const [hasStoredWebhook, setHasStoredWebhook] = useState(false)
    const [storedWebhookDisplay, setStoredWebhookDisplay] = useState('')
    const [barkUrl, setBarkUrl] = useState('')
    const [hasStoredBarkUrl, setHasStoredBarkUrl] = useState(false)
    const [storedBarkDisplay, setStoredBarkDisplay] = useState('')

    const [providerPreset, setProviderPreset] = useState('openai')
    const [customBaseUrl, setCustomBaseUrl] = useState('')
    const [deepThinkLlm, setDeepThinkLlm] = useState('')
    const [quickThinkLlm, setQuickThinkLlm] = useState('')
    const [maxDebateRounds, setMaxDebateRounds] = useState(1)
    const [maxRiskRounds, setMaxRiskRounds] = useState(1)
    const [serverFallbackEnabled, setServerFallbackEnabled] = useState(true)
    const [emailReportEnabled, setEmailReportEnabled] = useState(true)
    const [wecomReportEnabled, setWecomReportEnabled] = useState(true)
    const [barkReportEnabled, setBarkReportEnabled] = useState(true)
    const [configLoading, setConfigLoading] = useState(false)
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
    const [saveAllSaving, setSaveAllSaving] = useState(false)
    const [warmingUp, setWarmingUp] = useState(false)
    const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)
    const [saveMessage, setSaveMessage] = useState('设置已保存')
    const [configError, setConfigError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    const [warmupResults, setWarmupResults] = useState<RuntimeWarmupResult[]>([])
    const [warmupError, setWarmupError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    const [wecomWarmingUp, setWecomWarmingUp] = useState(false)
    const [wecomWarmupMessage, setWecomWarmupMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
    const [wecomWarmupError, setWecomWarmupError] = useState<string | null>(null)
    const [barkWarmingUp, setBarkWarmingUp] = useState(false)
    const [barkWarmupMessage, setBarkWarmupMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
    const [barkWarmupError, setBarkWarmupError] = useState<string | null>(null)

    // API Token states
    const [tokens, setTokens] = useState<UserToken[]>([])
    const [tokensLoading, setTokensLoading] = useState(false)
    const [newTokenName, setNewTokenName] = useState('')
    const [isCreatingToken, setIsCreatingToken] = useState(false)
    const [copiedTokenId, setCopiedTokenId] = useState<string | null>(null)
    const [newlyCreatedToken, setNewlyCreatedToken] = useState<string | null>(null)

    const selectedPreset = useMemo(
        () => PROVIDER_PRESETS.find((item) => item.id === providerPreset) || PROVIDER_PRESETS[0],
        [providerPreset],
    )

    const effectiveProvider = selectedPreset.provider
    const effectiveBaseUrl = selectedPreset.editableBaseUrl ? customBaseUrl.trim() : selectedPreset.baseUrl
    const currentApiKeyScope = providerKeyScope(effectiveProvider, effectiveBaseUrl)
    useEffect(() => {
        setWarmupResults([])
        setWarmupError(null)
    }, [providerPreset, customBaseUrl, deepThinkLlm, quickThinkLlm, llmApiKey])

    useEffect(() => {
        setWecomWarmupMessage(null)
        setWecomWarmupError(null)
    }, [wecomWebhook])

    useEffect(() => {
        setBarkWarmupMessage(null)
        setBarkWarmupError(null)
    }, [barkUrl])

    useEffect(() => {
        try {
            const stored = localStorage.getItem('tradingagents-settings')
            if (stored) {
                const s = JSON.parse(stored) as Record<string, unknown> & {
                    defaultAnalysts?: string[]
                }
                if ('apiUrl' in s) {
                    delete s.apiUrl
                    localStorage.setItem('tradingagents-settings', JSON.stringify(s))
                }
                if (s.defaultAnalysts) setDefaultAnalysts(s.defaultAnalysts)
                if (typeof s.customPrompt === 'string') setCustomPrompt(s.customPrompt)
            }
        } catch {}
    }, [])

    useEffect(() => {
        setConfigLoading(true)
        setConfigError(null)
        api.getConfig()
            .then(cfg => {
                setProviderPreset(inferPreset(cfg.llm_provider, cfg.backend_url))
                setCustomBaseUrl(cfg.backend_url || '')
                setDeepThinkLlm(cfg.deep_think_llm)
                setQuickThinkLlm(cfg.quick_think_llm)
                setMaxDebateRounds(cfg.max_debate_rounds)
                setMaxRiskRounds(cfg.max_risk_discuss_rounds)
                setHasStoredApiKey(!!cfg.has_api_key)
                setApiKeyScopes(cfg.api_key_scopes || [])
                setHasStoredWebhook(!!cfg.has_wecom_webhook)
                setStoredWebhookDisplay(cfg.wecom_webhook_display || '')
                setHasStoredBarkUrl(!!cfg.has_bark_url)
                setStoredBarkDisplay(cfg.bark_url_display || '')
                setServerFallbackEnabled(!!cfg.server_fallback_enabled)
                setEmailReportEnabled(cfg.email_report_enabled !== false)
                setWecomReportEnabled(cfg.wecom_report_enabled !== false)
                setBarkReportEnabled(cfg.bark_report_enabled !== false)
                if (Array.isArray(cfg.default_analysts) && cfg.default_analysts.length > 0) {
                    setDefaultAnalysts(cfg.default_analysts)
                }
            })
            .catch(err => {
                setConfigError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '无法连接到后端')
            })
            .finally(() => setConfigLoading(false))

        // Fetch tokens
        fetchTokens()
    }, [])

    const fetchTokens = async () => {
        setTokensLoading(true)
        try {
            const data = await api.getTokens()
            setTokens(data)
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('Failed to fetch tokens:', err)
        } finally {
            setTokensLoading(false)
        }
    }

    const handleProviderPresetChange = (presetId: string) => {
        const nextPreset = PROVIDER_PRESETS.find((preset) => preset.id === presetId)
        setProviderPreset(presetId)
        if (!nextPreset) return
        if (nextPreset.editableBaseUrl) {
            setCustomBaseUrl('')
        } else if (nextPreset.baseUrl) {
            setCustomBaseUrl(nextPreset.baseUrl)
        }
        if (nextPreset.defaultQuickModel) setQuickThinkLlm(nextPreset.defaultQuickModel)
        if (nextPreset.defaultDeepModel) setDeepThinkLlm(nextPreset.defaultDeepModel)
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        // [B-001-R1] Also recognize legacy openai: scoped keys for mimo providers
        let hasKey = apiKeyScopes.includes(currentApiKeyScope)
        if (!hasKey && currentApiKeyScope.startsWith('mimo:')) {
            const legacyScope = 'openai:' + currentApiKeyScope.slice('mimo:'.length)
            hasKey = apiKeyScopes.includes(legacyScope)
        }
        setHasStoredApiKey(hasKey)
    }, [apiKeyScopes, currentApiKeyScope])

    const handleCreateToken = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!newTokenName.trim()) return
        setIsCreatingToken(true)
        try {
            const created = await api.createToken({ name: newTokenName.trim() })
            setNewTokenName('')
            setNewlyCreatedToken(created.token || null)
            await fetchTokens()
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '创建 Token 失败')
        } finally {
            setIsCreatingToken(false)
        }
    }

    const handleDeleteToken = async (tokenId: string) => {
        if (!confirm('确定要吊销此 Token 吗？吊销后使用该 Token 的 API 请求将立即失效。')) return
        try {
            await api.deleteToken(tokenId)
            await fetchTokens()
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '吊销 Token 失败')
        }
    }

    const copyToClipboard = (text: string, id: string) => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
        setCopiedTokenId(id)
        setTimeout(() => setCopiedTokenId(null), 2000)
    }

    const persistLocalSettings = () => {
        localStorage.setItem('tradingagents-settings', JSON.stringify({
            defaultAnalysts,
            customPrompt,
        }))
        localStorage.setItem('ta-custom-prompt', customPrompt)
    }

    const buildRuntimeConfigPayload = (options?: { includeEmail?: boolean; includeWecom?: boolean; includeBark?: boolean }) => ({
        llm_provider: effectiveProvider,
        backend_url: effectiveBaseUrl || undefined,
        deep_think_llm: deepThinkLlm,
        quick_think_llm: quickThinkLlm,
        max_debate_rounds: maxDebateRounds,
        max_risk_discuss_rounds: maxRiskRounds,
        api_key: llmApiKey || undefined,
        ...(options?.includeWecom ? {
            wecom_webhook_url: wecomWebhook.trim() || undefined,
            wecom_report_enabled: wecomReportEnabled,
        } : {}),
        ...(options?.includeBark ? {
            bark_url: barkUrl.trim() || undefined,
            bark_report_enabled: barkReportEnabled,
        } : {}),
        ...(options?.includeEmail ? { email_report_enabled: emailReportEnabled } : {}),
        default_analysts: defaultAnalysts,
    })

    const showSavedMessage = (message: string) => {
        setSaveMessage(message)
        setSaved(true)
        setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000)
    }
    }

    const submitConfig = async (options?: { forceWarmup?: boolean; successMessage?: string; includeEmail?: boolean; includeWecom?: boolean; includeBark?: boolean }) => {
        persistLocalSettings()
        const { forceWarmup = false, successMessage = '设置已保存', includeEmail = true, includeWecom = false, includeBark = false } = options || {}
        const response = await api.updateConfig({
            ...buildRuntimeConfigPayload({ includeEmail, includeWecom, includeBark }),
            warmup: true,
            force_warmup: forceWarmup,
        })
        setHasStoredApiKey(!!response.has_api_key)
        setApiKeyScopes(response.current.api_key_scopes || [])
        setHasStoredWebhook(!!response.current.has_wecom_webhook)
        setStoredWebhookDisplay(response.current.wecom_webhook_display || '')
        setHasStoredBarkUrl(!!response.current.has_bark_url)
        setStoredBarkDisplay(response.current.bark_url_display || '')
        setWecomReportEnabled(response.current.wecom_report_enabled !== false)
        setBarkReportEnabled(response.current.bark_report_enabled !== false)
        setLlmApiKey('')
        setWecomWebhook('')
        setBarkUrl('')
        showSavedMessage(response.warmup?.message || successMessage)
        return response
    }

    const handleSaveAll = async () => {
        setSaveAllSaving(true)
        try {
            await submitConfig({ includeEmail: true, includeWecom: true, includeBark: true, successMessage: '全部设置已保存' })
            showSavedMessage('全部设置已保存')
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '保存全部设置失败')
        } finally {
            setSaveAllSaving(false)
        }
    }

    const handleWarmup = async () => {
        setWarmingUp(true)
        setWarmupError(null)
        setWarmupResults([])
        try {
            const response = await api.warmupConfig({
                ...buildRuntimeConfigPayload(),
                prompt: '你好',
            })
            setWarmupResults(response.results || [])
        } catch (err) {
            setWarmupError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Warmup 触发失败')
        } finally {
            setWarmingUp(false)
        }
    }
    const handleClearApiKey = async () => {
        if (!hasStoredApiKey) return
        setSaving(true)
        try {
            const response = await api.updateConfig({
                ...buildRuntimeConfigPayload(),
                clear_api_key: true,
                warmup: false,
            })
            setHasStoredApiKey(!!response.has_api_key)
            setApiKeyScopes(response.current.api_key_scopes || [])
            setLlmApiKey('')
            setSaved(true)
            setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000)
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '清除密钥失败')
        } finally {
            setSaving(false)
        }
    }

    const handleClearWebhook = async () => {
        if (!hasStoredWebhook) return
        setSaving(true)
        try {
            const response = await api.updateConfig({ clear_wecom_webhook: true })
            setHasStoredWebhook(!!response.current.has_wecom_webhook)
            setStoredWebhookDisplay(response.current.wecom_webhook_display || '')
            setWecomWebhook('')
            setWecomWarmupMessage(null)
            setWecomWarmupError(null)
            showSavedMessage('企业微信机器人已清除')
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '清除企业微信机器人失败')
        } finally {
            setSaving(false)
        }
    }

    const handleClearBark = async () => {
        if (!hasStoredBarkUrl) return
        setSaving(true)
        try {
            const response = await api.updateConfig({ clear_bark_url: true })
            setHasStoredBarkUrl(!!response.current.has_bark_url)
            setStoredBarkDisplay(response.current.bark_url_display || '')
            setBarkUrl('')
            setBarkWarmupMessage(null)
            setBarkWarmupError(null)
            showSavedMessage('Bark 推送已清除')
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '清除 Bark 推送失败')
        } finally {
            setSaving(false)
        }
    }

    const handleWecomWarmup = async () => {
        setWecomWarmingUp(true)
        setWecomWarmupMessage(null)
        setWecomWarmupError(null)
        try {
            const response = await api.warmupWecom({
                wecom_webhook_url: wecomWebhook.trim() || undefined,
            })
            setWecomWarmupMessage(
                response.webhook_display
                    ? `${response.message}，目标：${response.webhook_display}`
                    : response.message
            )
        } catch (err) {
            setWecomWarmupError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Webhook 测试发送失败')
        } finally {
            setWecomWarmingUp(false)
        }
    }

    const handleBarkWarmup = async () => {
        setBarkWarmingUp(true)
        setBarkWarmupMessage(null)
        setBarkWarmupError(null)
        try {
            const response = await api.warmupBark({
                bark_url: barkUrl.trim() || undefined,
            })
            setBarkWarmupMessage(
                response.bark_url_display
                    ? `${response.message}，目标：${response.bark_url_display}`
                    : response.message
            )
        } catch (err) {
            setBarkWarmupError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Bark 测试发送失败')
        } finally {
            setBarkWarmingUp(false)
        }
    }

    const toggleAnalyst = (analyst: string) => {
        setDefaultAnalysts(prev =>
            prev.includes(analyst) ? prev.filter(a => a !== analyst) : [...prev, analyst]
        )
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <div>
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">系统设置</h1>
                <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">配置当前账户的分析参数与模型</p>
            </div>

            <div className="card space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Database className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-500" />
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">模型接入</h2>
                    {configLoading && <Loader2 className="ml-auto w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
                </div>

                {configError && (
                    <p className="text-sm text-amber-500">⚠ {configError}（显示本地默认值）</p>
                )}

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                            模型厂商
                        </label>
                        <select
                            value={providerPreset}
                            onChange={e => handleProviderPresetChange(e.target.value)}
                            className="input w-full"
                            disabled={configLoading}
                        >
                            {PROVIDER_PRESETS.map((preset) => (
                                <option key={preset.id} value={preset.id}>{preset.label}</option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                            接入协议
                        </label>
                        <div className="input w-full flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/70 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                            <Link2 className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>{selectedPreset.protocol}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {(selectedPreset.baseUrl || selectedPreset.editableBaseUrl) && (
                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                                Base URL
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={selectedPreset.editableBaseUrl ? customBaseUrl : selectedPreset.baseUrl}
                                onChange={e => setCustomBaseUrl(e.target.value)}
                                className="input w-full"
                                disabled={configLoading || !selectedPreset.editableBaseUrl}
                                placeholder="https://your-openai-compatible-endpoint/v1"
                            />
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                {selectedPreset.editableBaseUrl
                                    ? '自定义 OpenAI 兼容服务需要自行填写 Base URL。'
                                    : '该厂商默认通过预设的 OpenAI 兼容地址接入，通常只需填写模型名和 API Key。'}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                            常规模型
                            <span className="ml-1 text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">用于意图识别、JSON 提取等轻量任务</span>
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={quickThinkLlm}
                            onChange={e => setQuickThinkLlm(e.target.value)}
                            className="input w-full"
                            placeholder={selectedPreset.defaultQuickModel || '例如：gpt-4.1-mini / deepseek-chat / moonshot-v1-8k'}
                            disabled={configLoading}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                            推理模型
                            <span className="ml-1 text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">用于深度分析、辩论等复杂任务</span>
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={deepThinkLlm}
                            onChange={e => setDeepThinkLlm(e.target.value)}
                            className="input w-full"
                            placeholder={selectedPreset.defaultDeepModel || '例如：gpt-4.1 / deepseek-reasoner / kimi-k2-0905-preview'}
                            disabled={configLoading}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2">
                            用户模型 Key
                        </label>
                        <div className="relative">
                            <Key className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                            <input
                                type="password"
                                value={llmApiKey}
                                onChange={e => setLlmApiKey(e.target.value)}
                                className="input w-full pl-10"
                                placeholder={hasStoredApiKey ? '已保存，留空则保持不变' : '输入你的模型 API Key'}
                                disabled={configLoading}
                            />
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                {serverFallbackEnabled
                                    ? '当前后端已开启公共模型回退：未填写个人 Key 时，可能仍会使用服务端默认模型配置。'
                                    : '当前后端已关闭公共模型回退：未填写个人 Key 时，将无法发起需要模型的分析任务。'}
                            </div>
                            {hasStoredApiKey && (
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={handleClearApiKey}
                                    disabled={saving || saveAllSaving}
                                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-600 disabled:opacity-50"
                                >
                                    <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                    清除密钥
                                </button>
                            )}
                        </div>
	                        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
	                            切换模型厂商时需要粘贴该厂商对应的 Key；留空会沿用当前已保存 Key。保存模型配置前，系统会先测试连通性；也可以直接点击下方按钮，发送\u201c你好\u201d来验证模型是否正常响应。
	                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="md:col-span-2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-50/80 dark:bg-slate-900/40 p-4 space-y-3">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                            <div>
                                <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">连通性测试</div>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    使用当前表单配置向模型发送“你好”，不会自动保存设置。
                                </p>
                            </div>
                            <button onClick={handleWarmup} disabled={saving || saveAllSaving || warmingUp || configLoading} className="btn-secondary inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                {warmingUp ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Flame className="w-4 h-4" />}
                                {warmingUp ? '测试中...' : '测试连接'}
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        {warmupError && (
                            <div className="rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-rose-600 dark:border-rose-900/60 dark:bg-rose-950/30 dark:text-rose-300">
                                {warmupError}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {warmupResults.length > 0 && (
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                                {warmupResults.map((item, index) => (
                                    <div
                                        key={`${item.model}-${index}`}
                                        className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-slate-700/80 bg-white dark:bg-slate-950/40 px-4 py-3"
                                    >
                                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            <span className="font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">{item.targets.join(' / ')}</span>
                                            <span>{item.model}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        {item.content && (
                                            <pre className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words font-sans text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
                                                {item.content}
                                            </pre>
                                        )}
                                        {item.error && (
                                            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-rose-500 dark:text-rose-300">{item.error}</p>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="card space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Database className="w-5 h-5 text-green-500" />
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">默认分析配置</h2>

  1303	def _build_runtime_config(overrides: Dict[str, Any], user_id: Optional[str] = None, db: Optional[Session] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1304	    config = deepcopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
  1305	    server_fallback_enabled = os.getenv("ALLOW_SERVER_LLM_FALLBACK", "1").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
  1306	    config["server_fallback_enabled"] = server_fallback_enabled
  1307	
  1308	    # Security: filter request overrides to allowlist only
  1309	    overrides = {k: v for k, v in overrides.items() if k in _CONFIG_OVERRIDES_ALLOWLIST}
  1310	
  1311	    # Apply global config overrides (from PATCH /v1/config)
  1312	    if _global_config_overrides:
  1313	        config = _deep_merge(config, dict(_global_config_overrides))
  1314	    
  1315	    # Fetch user specific overrides from DB (pass db to reuse caller's session)
  1316	    user_overrides = _user_config_overrides(user_id, db=db)
  1317	
  1318	    # ── Critical: Filter out empty strings before merging ──
  1319	    # This prevents an empty DB field from wiping out an Env Var default.
  1320	    filtered_user_overrides = {k: v for k, v in user_overrides.items() if v not in (None, "", [])}
  1321	    filtered_request_overrides = {k: v for k, v in overrides.items() if v not in (None, "", [])}
  1322	
  1323	    if filtered_user_overrides:
  1324	        config = _deep_merge(config, filtered_user_overrides)
  1325	    if filtered_request_overrides:
  1326	        config = _deep_merge(config, filtered_request_overrides)
  1327	
  1328	    config["llm_provider"] = auth_service.canonicalize_llm_provider(
  1329	        config.get("llm_provider"),
  1330	        config.get("backend_url"),
  1331	    )
  1332	
  1333	    # ── Intelligent fallback between models ──
  1334	    # If one is provided but the other is missing (even after env var merge), cross-fill.
  1335	    quick = config.get("quick_think_llm")
  1336	    deep = config.get("deep_think_llm")
  1337	
  1338	    if not deep and quick:
  1339	        config["deep_think_llm"] = quick
  1340	    if not quick and deep:
  1341	        config["quick_think_llm"] = deep
  1342	
  1343	    # UI/user configs currently expose quick/deep only. When a user switches to
  1344	    # another OpenAI-compatible endpoint, keep hidden tiers on the same endpoint
  1345	    # by mapping mid/ultra to deep instead of leaking env-level model names.
  1346	    if filtered_user_overrides or filtered_request_overrides:
  1347	        config["mid_think_llm"] = config.get("deep_think_llm") or config.get("quick_think_llm")
  1348	        config["ultra_think_llm"] = config.get("deep_think_llm") or config.get("quick_think_llm")
  1349	
  1350	    if user_id and db is not None and auth_service.has_user_provider_keys(db, user_id):
  1351	        config["api_key"] = auth_service.get_user_provider_api_key(
  1352	            db,
  1353	            user_id,
  1354	            config.get("llm_provider"),
  1355	            config.get("backend_url"),
  1356	        ) or ""
  1357	
  1358	    return config
  1359

  4270	        return True
  4271	    if updates.api_key:
  4272	        return True
  4273	    before = before_cfg.model_dump()
  4274	    after = after_cfg.model_dump()
  4275	    return any(before.get(key) != after.get(key) for key in _CONFIG_MODEL_KEYS)

  4280	    user_id: str,
  4281	    db: Session,
  4282	) -> Dict[str, Any]:

  4286	        if value is not None:
  4287	            config[key] = value

  4291	        config.get("backend_url"),

  4314	        elif auth_service.has_user_provider_keys(db, user_id):
  4315	            config["api_key"] = ""

  4459	def _config_response_for_user(user: Optional[UserDB], db: Session) -> UserRuntimeConfigResponse:
  4460	    cfg = _build_runtime_config({}, user_id=user.id if user else None, db=db)
  4461	    user_cfg = auth_service.get_user_llm_config(db, user.id) if user else None
  4462	    current_api_key_scope = auth_service.normalize_provider_key_scope(cfg.get("llm_provider"), cfg.get("backend_url"))
  4463	    api_key_scopes = auth_service.list_user_provider_key_scopes(db, user.id) if user else []
  4464	    api_key = auth_service.get_user_provider_api_key(
  4465	        db,
  4466	        user.id,
  4467	        cfg.get("llm_provider"),
  4468	        cfg.get("backend_url"),
  4469	    ) if user else None
  4470	    if not api_key and not api_key_scopes:
  4471	        api_key = auth_service.decrypt_secret_with_fallback(getattr(user_cfg, "api_key_encrypted", None))
  4472	    webhook_url = auth_service.decrypt_secret_with_fallback(getattr(user_cfg, "wecom_webhook_encrypted", None))
  4473	    bark_url = auth_service.decrypt_secret_with_fallback(getattr(user_cfg, "bark_url_encrypted", None))
  4474	    return UserRuntimeConfigResponse(
  4475	        llm_provider=cfg["llm_provider"],
  4476	        deep_think_llm=cfg["deep_think_llm"],
  4477	        quick_think_llm=cfg["quick_think_llm"],
  4478	        backend_url=cfg["backend_url"],
  4479	        max_debate_rounds=cfg["max_debate_rounds"],
  4480	        max_risk_discuss_rounds=cfg["max_risk_discuss_rounds"],
  4481	        has_api_key=bool(api_key),
  4482	        has_wecom_webhook=bool(webhook_url),
  4483	        wecom_webhook_display=_mask_wecom_webhook(webhook_url),
  4484	        has_bark_url=bool(bark_url),
  4485	        bark_url_display=_mask_bark_url(bark_url),
  4486	        server_fallback_enabled=bool(cfg.get("server_fallback_enabled", True)),
  4487	        email_report_enabled=user.email_report_enabled if user and hasattr(user, 'email_report_enabled') else True,
  4488	        wecom_report_enabled=user.wecom_report_enabled if user and hasattr(user, "wecom_report_enabled") else True,
  4489	        bark_report_enabled=user.bark_report_enabled if user and hasattr(user, "bark_report_enabled") else True,
  4490	        default_analysts=json.loads(user_cfg.default_analysts) if user_cfg and user_cfg.default_analysts else ["market", "social", "news", "fundamentals", "macro", "smart_money", "volume_price"],
  4491	        current_api_key_scope=current_api_key_scope,
  4492	        api_key_scopes=api_key_scopes,
  4493	    )

  4496	@app.post("/v1/auth/request-code")
  4497	def request_login_code(request: AuthRequestCodeRequest):
  4498	    email = auth_service.normalize_email(request.email)
  4499	    if not re.match(r"^[^@\s]+@[^@\s.]+\.[^@\s.]+$", email):
  4500	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="邮箱格式不正确")

"""Xiaomi MiMo LLM client adapter.

Supports mimo-v2.5 and mimo-v2.5-pro via OpenAI-compatible endpoints.
Two endpoint variants:
  - Standard:  https://api.xiaomimimo.com/v1  (model prefix: xiaomi/)
  - Token Plan: https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1 (no prefix)
"""

import logging
import os
from typing import Any, Optional

from .base_client import BaseLLMClient
from .openai_client import UnifiedChatOpenAI
from .rate_limiter import invoke_with_retry, ainvoke_with_retry
from .validators import validate_model

_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Endpoints
MIMO_STANDARD_URL = "https://api.xiaomimimo.com/v1"
MIMO_TOKEN_PLAN_URL = "https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1"

# Model families that support extended reasoning
MIMO_REASONING_MODELS = {"mimo-v2.5-pro"}

# All known MiMo model names (both endpoints)
MIMO_MODELS = {
    # Token Plan models
    "mimo-v2.5",
    "mimo-v2.5-pro",
    # Standard endpoint models (xiaomi/ prefix)
    "xiaomi/mimo-v2-flash",
    "xiaomi/mimo-v2-pro",
    "xiaomi/mimo-v2.5",
    "xiaomi/mimo-v2.5-pro",
}


class MiMoClient(BaseLLMClient):
    """Client for Xiaomi MiMo models (OpenAI-compatible)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: str,
        base_url: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(model, base_url, **kwargs)

    @property
    def _effective_base_url(self) -> str:
        """Resolve base URL: explicit > env > token-plan default."""
        if self.base_url:
            return self.base_url
        return MIMO_TOKEN_PLAN_URL

    def get_llm(self) -> Any:
        """Return configured ChatOpenAI instance for MiMo."""
        target_url = self._effective_base_url
        llm_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {"model": self.model}

        # Reasoning models don't support temperature
        if not self._is_reasoning_model(self.model):
            llm_kwargs["temperature"] = self.kwargs.get("temperature", 0)

        llm_kwargs["max_retries"] = 0
        llm_kwargs["timeout"] = self.kwargs.get("timeout", 300.0)

        _logger.info(
            "[LLM Client] Init MiMo (%s) at %s (Timeout=%ss)",
            self.model,
            target_url,
            llm_kwargs["timeout"],
        )

        llm_kwargs["base_url"] = target_url
        api_key = os.environ.get("TA_API_KEY") or os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY")
        if api_key:
            llm_kwargs["api_key"] = api_key

        for key in ("api_key", "callbacks", "reasoning_effort"):
            if key in self.kwargs:
                llm_kwargs[key] = self.kwargs[key]

        return UnifiedChatOpenAI(**llm_kwargs)

    def validate_model(self) -> bool:
        return validate_model("mimo", self.model)

    @staticmethod
    def _is_reasoning_model(model: str) -> bool:
        """Check if the MiMo model supports extended reasoning."""
        m = model.lower().split("/")[-1]  # strip xiaomi/ prefix
        return m in MIMO_REASONING_MODELS

from typing import Optional

from .base_client import BaseLLMClient
from .openai_client import OpenAIClient
from .anthropic_client import AnthropicClient
from .google_client import GoogleClient
from .mimo_client import MiMoClient


def create_llm_client(
    provider: str,
    model: str,
    base_url: Optional[str] = None,
    **kwargs,
) -> BaseLLMClient:
    """Create an LLM client for the specified provider.

    Args:
        provider: LLM provider (openai, anthropic, google, xai, ollama, openrouter, mimo)
        model: Model name/identifier
        base_url: Optional base URL for API endpoint
        **kwargs: Additional provider-specific arguments

    Returns:
        Configured BaseLLMClient instance

    Raises:
        ValueError: If provider is not supported
    """
    provider_lower = provider.lower()

    if provider_lower in ("openai", "ollama", "openrouter"):
        return OpenAIClient(model, base_url, provider=provider_lower, **kwargs)

    if provider_lower == "xai":
        return OpenAIClient(model, base_url, provider="xai", **kwargs)

    if provider_lower == "anthropic":
        return AnthropicClient(model, base_url, **kwargs)

    if provider_lower == "google":
        return GoogleClient(model, base_url, **kwargs)

    if provider_lower == "mimo":
        return MiMoClient(model, base_url, **kwargs)

    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported LLM provider: {provider}")

  3357	async def _ai_extract_symbol_and_date_streaming(
  3358	    text: str, config: Dict[str, Any], job_id: str
  3359	) -> tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], List[str], List[str], List[str], Dict[str, Any]]:
  3360	    """
  3361	    Async streaming version of _ai_extract_symbol_and_date.
  3362	    Emits agent.token events so the frontend can show streaming output during extraction.
  3363	    """
  3364	    from tradingagents.llm_clients.factory import create_llm_client
  3365	    import json as _json
  3366	
  3367	    today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
  3368	    fast_symbol, fast_date = _extract_symbol_and_date(text)
  3369	    llm_name: Optional[str] = None
  3370	    llm_date: Optional[str] = None
  3371	    llm_horizons: List[str] = ["short"]
  3372	    llm_focus_areas: List[str] = []
  3373	    llm_specific_questions: List[str] = []
  3374	    llm_user_context: Dict[str, Any] = {}
  3375	
  3376	    try:
  3377	        client = create_llm_client(
  3378	            provider=config.get("llm_provider", "openai"),
  3379	            model=config.get("quick_think_llm"),
  3380	            base_url=config.get("backend_url"),
  3381	            api_key=config.get("api_key"),
  3382	        )
  3383	        prompt = f"""你是金融数据助手。从用户消息中提取以下字段并以 JSON 输出。
  3384	
  3385	字段说明：
  3386	- stock_name：用户提到的公司名称或股票代码原文（如"华盛天成"、"贵州茅台"、"600519"、"AAPL"）；美股直接填 ticker。
  3387	- date：YYYY-MM-DD 格式。今天是 {today}，如未提及则填今天。
  3388	- horizons：分析周期，只能选一个：
  3389	  * 用户明确提到"中线/中期/几个月/季度/长期/趋势投资"→ ["medium"]
  3390	  * 其他所有情况（含未提及）→ ["short"]
  3391	- focus_areas：用户关注的分析维度关键词列表，如 ["技术面", "资金面", "业绩"]，未提及则 []。
  3392	- specific_questions：用户提出的具体问题列表，如 ["近期有无催化剂？", "主力是否出货？"]，未提及则 []。
  3393	- user_context：从自然语言中提取的账户与约束对象。若未提及返回 {{}}。可包含：
  3394	  * objective：建仓 / 加仓 / 减仓 / 止损 / 观察 / 持有处理
  3395	  * risk_profile：保守 / 平衡 / 激进
  3396	  * investment_horizon：短线 / 波段 / 中线 / 长期
  3397	  * cash_available / current_position / current_position_pct / average_cost / max_loss_pct：数字
  3398	  * constraints：字符串数组
  3399	  * user_notes：仅保留重要但未能结构化归类的信息
  3400	
  3401	仅输出 JSON，不要任何其他文字：
  3402	{{"stock_name": "...", "date": "YYYY-MM-DD", "horizons": ["short"], "focus_areas": [], "specific_questions": [], "user_context": {{}}}}
  3403	
  3404	如果无法识别股票标的：{{"stock_name": null, "date": null, "horizons": ["short"], "focus_areas": [], "specific_questions": [], "user_context": {{}}}}
  3405	
  3406	用户消息："{text}"
  3407	"""
  3408	        llm = client.get_llm()
  3409	        _log(f"[LLM Debug] Streaming StockExtract with model: {getattr(llm, 'model_name', 'unknown')}")
  3410	
  3411	        full_content = ""
  3412	        async for chunk in llm.astream(prompt):
  3413	            token = chunk.content if hasattr(chunk, "content") else str(chunk)
  3414	            full_content += token
  3415	            if token:
  3416	                _emit_job_event(job_id, "agent.token", {
  3417	                    "agent": "意图解析",
  3418	                    "report": "stock_extract",
  3419	                    "token": token,
  3420	                })
  3421	
  3422	        _log(f"[LLM Debug] StockExtract response: {full_content[:200]}")
  3423	        m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", full_content, re.DOTALL)
  3424	        if m:
  3425	            data = _json.loads(m.group(0))
  3426	            llm_name = (data.get("stock_name") or "").strip() or None
  3427	            llm_date = data.get("date") or today
  3428	            llm_horizons = data.get("horizons") or ["short"]
  3429	            llm_focus_areas = data.get("focus_areas") or []
  3430	            llm_specific_questions = data.get("specific_questions") or []
  3431	            llm_user_context = normalize_user_context(data.get("user_context") or {})
  3432	    except Exception as e:
  3433	        _log(f"[StockExtract streaming] LLM failed: {e}")
  3434	
  3435	    if not llm_name:
  3436	        if fast_symbol:
  3437	            _log(f"[StockExtract] LLM 未返回 stock_name，使用 regex 兜底: {fast_symbol}")
  3438	            return fast_symbol, fast_date or today, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context
  3439	        return None, None, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context
  3440	
  3441	    _log(f"[StockExtract] extracted name='{llm_name}', date={llm_date}, horizons={llm_horizons}")
  3442	    if re.match(r"^\d{6}(?:\.(?:SH|SZ|SS))?$", llm_name, re.IGNORECASE) or re.match(r"^[A-Za-z]{1,6}(\.[A-Za-z]+)?$", llm_name):
  3443	        symbol = _normalize_symbol(llm_name)
  3444	        if symbol:
  3445	            return symbol, llm_date, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context
  3446	
  3447	    local_code = await asyncio.to_thread(_search_cn_stock_by_name, llm_name)
  3448	    if local_code:
  3449	        return local_code, llm_date, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context
  3450	
  3451	    fallback = _normalize_symbol(llm_name)
  3452	    if fallback and re.search(r"\d{6}|[A-Za-z]{2,}", fallback):
  3453	        return fallback, llm_date, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context
  3454	
  3455	    if fast_symbol:
  3456	        _log(f"[StockExtract] LLM 名 '{llm_name}' 无法解析为代码，使用 regex 兜底: {fast_symbol}")
  3457	        return fast_symbol, llm_date or fast_date or today, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context
  3458	
  3459	    return None, llm_date, llm_horizons, llm_focus_areas, llm_specific_questions, llm_user_context

  3465	    """
  3466	    Single-LLM extraction: stock name, date, horizons, focus_areas, specific_questions.

  3468	    Returns (symbol, date, horizons, focus_areas, specific_questions, inferred_user_context).
  3469	    """

  3472

  3474	    fast_symbol, fast_date = _extract_symbol_and_date(text)
  3475

  3484	            provider=config.get("llm_provider", "openai"),
  3485	            model=config.get("quick_think_llm"),
  3486	            base_url=config.get("backend_url"),
  3487	            api_key=config.get("api_key"),
  3488	        )
  3489	        prompt = f"""你是金融数据助手。从用户消息中提取以下字段并以 JSON 输出。
  3490	
  3491	字段说明：
  3492	- stock_name：用户提到的公司名称或股票代码原文（如"华盛天成"、"贵州茅台"、"600519"、"AAPL"）；美股直接填 ticker。
  3493	- date：YYYY-MM-DD 格式。今天是 {today}，如未提及则填今天。
  3494	- horizons：分析周期，只能选一个：
  3495	  * 用户明确提到"中线/中期/几个月/季度/长期/趋势投资"→ ["medium"]
  3496	  * 其他所有情况（含未提及）→ ["short"]
  3497	- focus_areas：用户关注的分析维度关键词列表，如 ["技术面", "资金面", "业绩"]，未提及则 []。
  3498	- specific_questions：用户提出的具体问题列表，如 ["近期有无催化剂？", "主力是否出货？"]，未提及则 []。
  3499	- user_context：从自然语言中提取的账户与约束对象。若未提及返回 {{}}。可包含：
  3500	  * objective：建仓 / 加仓 / 减仓 / 止损 / 观察 / 持有处理
  3501	  * risk_profile：保守 / 平衡 / 激进
  3502	  * investment_horizon：短线 / 波段 / 中线 / 长期
  3503	  * cash_available / current_position / current_position_pct / average_cost / max_loss_pct：数字
  3504	  * constraints：字符串数组
  3505	  * user_notes：仅保留重要但未能结构化归类的信息
  4320	    before_cfg: UserRuntimeConfigResponse,
  4321	    pending_cfg: Dict[str, Any],
  4322	    updates: UserRuntimeConfigUpdateRequest,
  4323	) -> bool:
  4324	    if updates.clear_api_key:
  4325	        return False
  4326	    if updates.api_key:
  4327	        return True
  4328	    if not str(pending_cfg.get("api_key") or "").strip():
  4329	        return False
  4330	
  4331	    before = before_cfg.model_dump()
  4332	    for key in _CONFIG_MODEL_KEYS:
  4333	        next_value = getattr(updates, key, None)
  4334	        if next_value is not None and next_value != before.get(key):
  4335	            return True
  4336	    return False

  4339	def _probe_runtime_config(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
  4340	    from tradingagents.llm_clients.factory import create_llm_client
  4341	
  4342	    provider = str(config.get("llm_provider") or "openai")
  4343	    base_url = config.get("backend_url")
  4344	    api_key = str(config.get("api_key") or "").strip()
  4345	    model = str(config.get("quick_think_llm") or config.get("deep_think_llm") or "").strip()
  4346	
  4347	    if not model or not api_key:
  4348	        return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "missing_model_or_key"}
  4349	
  4350	    try:
  4351	        client = create_llm_client(
  4352	            provider=provider,
  4353	            model=model,
  4354	            base_url=base_url,
  4355	            api_key=api_key,
  4356	            timeout=_CONFIG_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
  4357	            max_retries=0,
  4358	        )
  4359	        llm = client.get_llm()
  4360	        response = llm.invoke(_CONFIG_PROBE_PROMPT)
  4361	        raw = response if isinstance(response, str) else getattr(response, "content", str(response))
  4362	        preview = str(raw).strip().replace("\n", " ")[:80] or "<empty>"
  4363	        return {"status": "ok", "model": model, "preview": preview}
  4364	    except Exception as exc:
  4365	        detail = str(exc).strip()
  4366	        lowered = detail.lower()
  4367	        if "401" in lowered or "invalid authentication" in lowered or "authenticationerror" in lowered:
  4368	            raise HTTPException(
  4369	                status_code=400,
  4370	                detail="模型 Key 验证失败：上游返回 401 Invalid Authentication，请检查 API Key 是否正确。",
  4371	            ) from exc
  4372	        raise HTTPException(
  4373	            status_code=400,
  4374	            detail=f"模型连接验证失败：{detail[:200] or 'unknown error'}",
  4375	        ) from exc
  4376

   690	def _announcements_file() -> Path:
   691	    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "announcements.json"
   692	
   693	
   694	def _load_latest_announcement() -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   695	    path = _announcements_file()
   696	    if not path.exists():
   697	        return None
   698	    try:
   699	        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   700	    except Exception as exc:
   701	        _log(f"[Announcements] Failed to read {path.name}: {exc}")
   702	        return None
   703	
   704	    announcements = raw.get("announcements") if isinstance(raw, dict) else raw
   705	    if not isinstance(announcements, list):
   706	        return None
   707	
   708	    for item in announcements:
   709	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
   710	            continue
   711	        if item.get("active", True) is False:
   712	            continue
   713	        return item
   714	    return None

  4206	def _mask_secret_value(value: Optional[str], *, head: int = 4, tail: int = 4) -> Optional[str]:
  4207	    normalized = str(value or "").strip()
  4208	    if not normalized:
  4209	        return None
  4210	    if len(normalized) <= head + tail:
  4211	        return "*" * max(6, len(normalized))
  4212	    return f"{normalized[:head]}{'*' * max(6, len(normalized) - head - tail)}{normalized[-tail:]}"

   251	def calculate_data_completeness(
   252	    has_market_data: bool = False,
   253	    has_sentiment_data: bool = False,
   254	    has_news_data: bool = False,
   255	    has_fundamentals_data: bool = False,
   256	    has_smart_money_data: bool = False,
   257	    has_volume_price_data: bool = False,
   258	    has_user_context: bool = False,
   259	    has_position_data: bool = False,
   260	) -> int:
   261	    """
   262	    计算数据完整度（0-100%）。
   263	    根据可用数据源的数量计算。
   264	    """
   265	    total_fields = 8
   266	    available_fields = sum([
   267	        has_market_data,
   268	        has_sentiment_data,
   269	        has_news_data,
   270	        has_fundamentals_data,
   780	    score += {"favorable": 20, "neutral": 10, "unfavorable": 0}.get(risk_reward_ratio, 5)
   781	    score += {"good": 15, "acceptable": 8, "poor": 0}.get(entry_quality, 3)
   782	
   783	    if event_risk_active:
   784	        score = int(score * 0.6)
   785	
   786	    # [Fix-3] Apply caps
   787	    caps = []
   788	    if not gate_passed:
   789	        caps.append(60)
   790	    if final_direction_bearish:
   791	        caps.append(60)
   792	    if trader_disallows_buy:
   793	        caps.append(50)
   794	    if data_completeness < 80:
   795	        caps.append(70)
   796	    if position_status == "unknown":
   797	        caps.append(70)
   798	
   799	    if caps:
   800	        upper = min(caps)
   801	        score = min(score, upper)
   802	
   803	    return min(100, max(0, score))
   804	
   805	
   806	def _sanitize_short_selling_text(text: str) -> tuple:
   807	    """[Fix-9] Replace A-share short-selling language with appropriate alternatives.
   808	
   809	    When can_short=false (A-share default), replace:
   810	    - 做空策略 → 看空信号
   811	    - 空头开仓 → 离场信号
   812	    - 若做空 → 若确认偏空
   813	    - etc.
   814	
   815	    [C-003-R1] English patterns are matched case-insensitively so that
   816	    uppercase LLM action tokens like "OPEN SHORT" / "Short Selling" are
   817	    caught.
   818	
   819	    Returns (sanitized_text, list_of_changes)
   820	    """
   821	    changes = []
   822	    result = text
   823	    # [C-003-R1] Chinese patterns: literal match.
   824	    for pattern, replacement in _SHORT_SELLING_PATTERNS_CN:
   825	        matches = list(re.finditer(pattern, result))
   826	        if matches:
   827	            changes.append(f'"{matches[0].group(0)}" → "{replacement}"')
   828	            result = re.sub(pattern, replacement, result)
   829	    # [C-003-R1] English patterns: case-insensitive match.
   830	    for pattern, replacement in _SHORT_SELLING_PATTERNS_EN:
   831	        matches = list(re.finditer(pattern, result, re.IGNORECASE))
   832	        if matches:
   833	            changes.append(f'"{matches[0].group(0)}" → "{replacement}"')
   834	            result = re.sub(pattern, replacement, result, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
   835	    return result, changes
   836

  1303	def _build_runtime_config(overrides: Dict[str, Any], user_id: Optional[str] = None, db: Optional[Session] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1304	    config = deepcopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
  1305	    server_fallback_enabled = os.getenv("ALLOW_SERVER_LLM_FALLBACK", "1").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
  1306	    config["server_fallback_enabled"] = server_fallback_enabled
  1307	
  1308	    # Security: filter request overrides to allowlist only
  1309	    overrides = {k: v for k, v in overrides.items() if k in _CONFIG_OVERRIDES_ALLOWLIST}
  1310	
  1311	    # Apply global config overrides (from PATCH /v1/config)
  1312	    if _global_config_overrides:
  1313	        config = _deep_merge(config, dict(_global_config_overrides))
  1314	    
  1315	    # Fetch user specific overrides from DB (pass db to reuse caller's session)
  1316	    user_overrides = _user_config_overrides(user_id, db=db)
  1317	
  1318	    # ── Critical: Filter out empty strings before merging ──
  1319	    # This prevents an empty DB field from wiping out an Env Var default.
  1320	    filtered_user_overrides = {k: v for k, v in user_overrides.items() if v not in (None, "", [])}
  1321	    filtered_request_overrides = {k: v for k, v in overrides.items() if v not in (None, "", [])}
  1322	
  1323	    if filtered_user_overrides:
  1324	        config = _deep_merge(config, filtered_user_overrides)
  1325	    if filtered_request_overrides:
  1326	        config = _deep_merge(config, filtered_request_overrides)
  1327	
  1328	    # ── Intelligent fallback between models ──
  1329	    # If one is provided but the other is missing (even after env var merge), cross-fill.
  1330	    quick = config.get("quick_think_llm")
  1331	    deep = config.get("deep_think_llm")
  1332	
  1333	    if not deep and quick:
  1334	        config["deep_think_llm"] = quick
  1335	    if not quick and deep:
  1336	        config["quick_think_llm"] = deep
  1337	
  1338	    # UI/user configs currently expose quick/deep only. When a user switches to
  1339	    # another OpenAI-compatible endpoint, keep hidden tiers on the same endpoint
  1340	    # by mapping mid/ultra to deep instead of leaking env-level model names.
  1341	    if filtered_user_overrides or filtered_request_overrides:
  1342	        config["mid_think_llm"] = config.get("deep_think_llm") or config.get("quick_think_llm")
  1343	        config["ultra_think_llm"] = config.get("deep_think_llm") or config.get("quick_think_llm")
  1344	
  1345	    if user_id and db is not None and auth_service.has_user_provider_keys(db, user_id):
  1346	        config["api_key"] = auth_service.get_user_provider_api_key(
  1347	            db,
  1348	            user_id,
  1349	            config.get("llm_provider"),
  1350	            config.get("backend_url"),
  1351	        ) or ""
  1352	
  1353	    return config

    33	function inferPreset(llmProvider: string, backendUrl: string): string {
    34	    const normalizedProvider = (llmProvider || '').toLowerCase()
    35	    const normalizedUrl = (backendUrl || '').replace(/\/$/, '')
    36	    const matched = PROVIDER_PRESETS.find((preset) => {
    37	        if (preset.provider !== normalizedProvider) return false
    38	        if (!preset.baseUrl && preset.id !== 'custom-openai') return true
    39	        return preset.baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '') === normalizedUrl
    40	    })
    41	    if (matched) return matched.id
    42	    if (normalizedProvider === 'openai') return 'custom-openai'
    43	    return normalizedProvider || 'openai'
    44	}
    45	
    46	function providerKeyScope(llmProvider: string, backendUrl: string): string {
    47	    const provider = (llmProvider || 'openai').trim().toLowerCase() || 'openai'
    48	    const normalizedUrl = (backendUrl || '').trim().replace(/\/$/, '')
    49	    return normalizedUrl ? `${provider}:${normalizedUrl}` : provider
    50	}

   158	# ── Notification ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   159	
   160	async def _send_scheduled_report_notifications(
   161	    user_id: str, report_id: str, symbol: str
   162	) -> None:
   163	    """Send configured scheduled report notifications (email & WeCom).
   164	
   165	    Bark is sent from the API report-persist hook so every report creation
   166	    path behaves consistently and scheduled jobs do not double-push.
   167	    """
   168	    try:
   169	        from api.services.email_report_service import send_report_email_with_retry
   170	        from api.services.wecom_notification_service import send_report_message_with_retry
   171	
   172	        def _load_notification_targets():
   173	            email_user = None
   174	            report_to_send = None
   175	            webhook_url = None
   176	            wecom_report_enabled = True
   177	            with get_db_ctx() as db:
   178	                user = db.query(UserDB).filter(UserDB.id == user_id).first()
   179	                report = db.query(ReportDB).filter(ReportDB.id == report_id).first()
   180	                user_cfg = auth_service.get_user_llm_config(db, user_id)
   181	                webhook_url = auth_service.decrypt_secret(
   182	                    getattr(user_cfg, "wecom_webhook_encrypted", None)
   183	                )
   184	                if report:
   185	                    db.expunge(report)
   186	                    report_to_send = report
   187	                if user:
   188	                    wecom_report_enabled = getattr(user, "wecom_report_enabled", True)
   189	                    if getattr(user, "email_report_enabled", True):
   190	                        db.expunge(user)
   191	                        email_user = user
   192	            return email_user, report_to_send, webhook_url, wecom_report_enabled
   193	
   194	        email_user, report_to_send, webhook_url, wecom_report_enabled = (
   195	            await asyncio.to_thread(_load_notification_targets)
   196	        )
   197	        if email_user and report_to_send:
   198	            _log(f"[Scheduler] Sending email report for {symbol} to {email_user.email}")
   199	            _create_tracked_task(
   200	                send_report_email_with_retry(email_user, report_to_send),
   201	                label=f"Email notification task ({symbol})",
   202	            )
   203	        if report_to_send and webhook_url and wecom_report_enabled:
   204	            _log(f"[Scheduler] Sending WeCom report for {symbol}")
   205	            _create_tracked_task(
   206	                send_report_message_with_retry(report_to_send, webhook_url),
   207	                label=f"WeCom notification task ({symbol})",
   208	            )
   209	    except Exception as e:
   210	        logger.warning(f"[Scheduler] Notification send failed for {symbol}: {e}")
   211

   454	async def _send_report_bark_notification(
   455	    user_id: Optional[str],
   456	    report_id: str,
   457	    symbol: str,
   458	    *,
   459	    source: str = "report_saved",
   460	) -> None:
   461	    """Send Bark once a report is persisted, for all report creation paths."""
   462	    if not user_id or not report_id:
   463	        return
   464	    try:
   465	        from api.services.bark_notification_service import send_report_message_with_retry
   466	
   467	        bark_url = None
   468	        report_to_send = None
   469	        with get_db_ctx() as db:
   470	            user = db.query(UserDB).filter(UserDB.id == user_id).first()
   471	            if not user or not getattr(user, "bark_report_enabled", True):
   472	                return
   473	            user_cfg = auth_service.get_user_llm_config(db, user_id)
   474	            bark_url = auth_service.decrypt_secret(getattr(user_cfg, "bark_url_encrypted", None))
   475	            if not bark_url:
   476	                return
   477	            report = db.query(ReportDB).filter(ReportDB.id == report_id).first()
   478	            if not report or report.status != "completed":
   479	                return
   480	            db.expunge(report)
   481	            report_to_send = report
   482	
   483	        if report_to_send and bark_url:
   484	            _log(f"[Bark] Sending report notification for {symbol} source={source}")
   485	            await send_report_message_with_retry(report_to_send, bark_url)
   486	    except Exception as exc:
   487	        logger.warning("[Bark] report notification failed for %s: %s", symbol, exc)

   281	# ── Single scheduled analysis execution ──────────────────────────────────────
   282	
   283	async def _run_scheduled_analysis_once(
   284	    task: dict,
   285	    requested_trade_date: str,
   286	    job_id: str,
   287	    *,
   288	    mark_schedule_run: bool,
   289	) -> None:
   290	    """Execute one scheduled analysis, optionally recording it as the daily run."""
   291	    task_id = task["id"]
   292	    user_id = task["user_id"]
   293	    symbol = task["symbol"]
   294	    horizon = task.get("horizon") or "short"
   295	
   296	    actual_trade_date = _resolve_scheduled_trade_date(requested_trade_date)
   297	    _log(f"[Scheduler] {symbol} trade_date={actual_trade_date} (requested={requested_trade_date})")
   298	
   299	    scheduled_context_token = set_scheduled_task_context(True)
   300	
   301	    def _build_request_sync():
   302	        with get_db_ctx() as db:
   303	            scheduled_user_context = task.get("manual_user_context") or _build_imported_user_context(
   304	                db, user_id, symbol
   305	            )
   306	            return _build_scheduled_analyze_request(
   307	                db=db,
   308	                user_id=user_id,
   309	                symbol=symbol,
   310	                horizon=horizon,
   311	                trade_date=actual_trade_date,
   312	                scheduled_user_context=scheduled_user_context,
   313	            )
   314	
   315	    def _record_success_sync():
   316	        with get_db_ctx() as db:
   317	            if mark_schedule_run:
   318	                scheduled_service.mark_run_success(db, task_id, requested_trade_date, job_id)
   319	            else:
   320	                scheduled_service.record_manual_test_result(db, task_id, "success", report_id=job_id)
   321	
   322	    def _record_failure_sync():
   323	        with get_db_ctx() as db:
   324	            if mark_schedule_run:
   325	                scheduled_service.mark_run_failed(db, task_id, requested_trade_date)
   326	            else:
   327	                scheduled_service.record_manual_test_result(db, task_id, "failed")
   328	    try:
   329	        async with _concurrency_slot(job_id, symbol):
   330	            req = await asyncio.to_thread(_build_request_sync)
   331	
   332	            await _run_job(
   333	                job_id,
   334	                req,
   335	                False,
   336	                True,
   337	                user_id,
   338	                "scheduled" if mark_schedule_run else "scheduled_manual",
   339	            )
   340	        job_state = _get_job(job_id)
   341	        if job_state.get("status") == "failed":
   342	            raise RuntimeError(job_state.get("error") or f"scheduled analysis job {job_id} failed")
   343	        await asyncio.to_thread(_record_success_sync)
   344	        _log(f"[Scheduler] Completed {symbol}")
   345	
   346	        await _send_scheduled_report_notifications(user_id, job_id, symbol)
   347	
   348	        # [B-002] OpenClaw callback: notify 主控 AI so it can decide whether to push to 飞书
   349	        await _send_openclaw_callback(
   350	            user_id, job_id, symbol, actual_trade_date, horizon,
   351	            "scheduled" if mark_schedule_run else "scheduled_manual",
   352	        )
   353	
   354	        # [M-010] Feishu webhook: auto-generate pending notification drafts
   355	        await _send_feishu_notification_draft(user_id, job_id, symbol)
   356	    except Exception as e:
   357	        logger.error(f"[Scheduler] Failed {symbol}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
   358	        try:
   359	            await asyncio.to_thread(_record_failure_sync)
   360	        except Exception as db_exc:
   361	            logger.error(f"[Scheduler] Could not record failure: {db_exc}")
   362	    finally:
   363	        reset_scheduled_task_context(scheduled_context_token)
   364	
   365	
   366	async def _run_scheduled_job(task: dict, trade_date: str):
   367	    """Execute a single scheduled analysis job.
   368	
   369	    Args:
   370	        task: dict with keys id, user_id, symbol, horizon (plain values,
   371	              not an ORM instance, to avoid DetachedInstanceError).
   372	        trade_date: YYYY-MM-DD string.
   373	    """
   374	    user_id = task["user_id"]
   375	    symbol = task["symbol"]
   376	
   377	    _log(f"[Scheduler] Running {symbol} for user={user_id}")
   378	    job_id = uuid4().hex
   379	    _log(f"[Scheduler] {symbol} runtime_tier=FULL_TA cost_risk=high estimated_calls=20 (user-initiated scheduled task)")  # [PERF-001] [PERF-004]
   380	    try:
   381	        await _run_scheduled_analysis_once(
   382	            task,
   383	            trade_date,
   384	            job_id,
   385	            mark_schedule_run=True,
   386	        )
   387	    finally:
   388	        get_job_store().delete_job(job_id)
   389
